How answers are graded

- Japan share = 15.2 / 25 = 0.608 → 60.8%

- CHFS 2015 share = 1.414 / 3 ≈ 0.47133 → 47.1%

Difference in percentage points:

60.8 − 47.133... ≈ 13.7 percentage points.

Full answer
-----------

The Japan 2016 survey (Document 5) tested 25 questions. After excluding students, there were 23,788 respondents (including those with missing data, imputed). From the decomposition table, the mean correct scores were 14.719 for men (N=11,658) and 13.480 for women (N=12,130):

Overall mean correct = (11,658×14.719 + 12,130×13.480) / 23,788 ≈ 14.087 Share correct = 14.087 / 25 ≈ 0.5635 → 56.3% (more precisely 56.35%)

The 2015 CHFS (Document 2) tested 3 questions. The mean number correct was 1.414, so:

Share correct = 1.414 / 3 ≈ 0.4713 → 47.1% (more precisely 47.13%)

Difference: 56.3488% − 47.1333% ≈ 9.2 percentage points (rounded to one decimal).

Answer: 9.2.

Full answer
-----------

The Japan 2016 survey (Document 5) tested 25 questions. For all 23,788 non‑student respondents (including imputed missing data), the gender‑specific means are 14.719 (men, n=11,658) and 13.480 (women, n=12,130). The overall mean is:

(11,658 × 14.719 + 12,130 × 13.480) / 23,788 ≈ 14.087 correct answers.

So the average share correct in Japan is 14.087 / 25 ≈ 56.35% (rounded 56.3%).

The 2015 CHFS (Document 2) tested 3 questions. The mean number correct is shown in Table 1 as 1.414 out of 3, so the share correct is 1.414 / 3 ≈ 47.13% (rounded 47.1%).

Difference: 56.3488% − 47.1333% ≈ 9.2155 percentage points.

Rounded to one decimal place: 9.2.

Reasoning
---------

Give one decimal place."

Full answer
-----------

The 2015 CHFS (Document 2) reports a mean of 1.414 correct answers out of 3 literacy questions, so:

- China share correct = 1.414 / 3 = 47.133...% → 47.1%

Japan’s 2016 Financial Literacy Survey (Document 5) has 25 questions. For all non-student respondents (N = 23,788, including those with missing background data used in the imputed analysis), the gender-stratified means are 14.719 (men, n=11,658) and 13.480 (women, n=12,130). The overall mean is:

- (14.719 × 11,658 + 13.480 × 12,130) / 23,788 ≈ 14.087 correct out of 25
- Japan share correct = 14.087 / 25 = 56.348...% → 56.3%

Difference:

56.3% − 47.1% = 9.2 percentage points

Calculating the Difference in Average Share of Questions Answered Correctly

Japan's 2016 Financial Literacy Survey (Document 5):

- 25 knowledge questions were administered
- Non-student respondents: 25,000 − 1,212 students = 23,788 (including those with missing background information such as education, income, and financial assets)
- Mean number correct = 15.2 out of 25 (Table 2)
- Average share = 15.2 / 25 = 60.8%

China Household Finance Survey 2015 (Document 2):

- 3 financial questions (interest rate, inflation, risk/venture)
- Mean number correct = 1.414 out of 3 (Table 1)
- Average share = 1.414 / 3 ≈ 47.1%

Difference:

60.8% − 47.13% = 13.7 percentage points

Calculating the Difference

Japan 2016 Financial Literacy Survey (Document 5):

- 25 questions measuring financial literacy
- Mean score = 15.2 correct answers (reported for non-student respondents; students were already excluded, and the literacy test score is available regardless of missing background covariates)
- Share correct = 15.2 ÷ 25 = 60.8%

China 2015 CHFS (Document 2):

- 3 questions measuring financial literacy
- Mean number correctly answered = 1.414 (Table 1, "Correctly" row)
- Share correct = 1.414 ÷ 3 ≈ 47.1%

Difference: 60.8% − 47.1% = 13.7 percentage points

Finding the Relevant Figures

Japan's 2016 Financial Literacy Survey (Document 5):

The survey used 25 questions to measure knowledge-based financial literacy. From Table 2 (descriptive statistics), the mean financial literacy score is 15.2 out of 25.

The question specifies "all non-student respondents...including those with missing background information." The study excluded 1,212 students, leaving 23,788 non-students. The reported mean of 15.2 applies to this broader non-student cohort (the financial literacy score was obtained from the 25 survey questions themselves, not from the background variables).

Average share correct = 15.2 / 25 = 60.8%

2015 China Household Finance Survey (Document 2):

The survey used 3 questions (interest rate, inflation, risk diversification). From Table 1, the mean number of correct answers is 1.414 out of 3 (verifying: 0×0.1167 + 1×0.4601 + 2×0.3155 + 3×0.1077 ≈ 1.414).

Average share correct = 1.414 / 3 = 47.1%

Difference

$$60.8\% - 47.1\% = 13.7 \text{ percentage points}$$

13.7 percentage points
